dau_choose_east_asian_estate_garden_leisure_04_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_choice_ready_east_asian_estate_garden_leisure_04 = yes }
        dau_initialize_effect = yes
        save_scope_as = owner
        dau_clear_temporary_effect = yes
        set_variable = { name = dau_best_main value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = dau_best_score value = 0 }
        dau_consider_east_asian_estate_garden_leisure_04 = yes
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = dau_candidate }
            domicile = { set_variable = { name = dau_status value = 3 } }
            dau_construct_selected_effect = yes
        }
        dau_clear_temporary_effect = yes
    }
}

        limit = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes dau_choice_ready_guard_tower_provosts_hall_01 = yes }
        dau_initialize_effect = yes
        save_scope_as = owner
        dau_clear_temporary_effect = yes
        set_variable = { name = dau_best_main value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = dau_best_score value = 0 }
        dau_consider_guard_tower_provosts_hall_01 = yes
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = dau_candidate }
            domicile = { set_variable = { name = dau_status value = 3 } }
            dau_construct_selected_effect = yes
        }
        dau_clear_temporary_effect = yes
    }
}

    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = { has_variable = dau_choice_pending }
            domicile = { NOT = { has_variable = dau_defer_external_camp } }
            OR = { dau_page_external_camp_0_trigger = yes dau_page_external_camp_1_trigger = yes }
        }
        set_variable = dau_choice_pending
        domicile = { save_scope_as = dau_choice_home set_variable = { name = dau_status value = 6 } }
        dau_open_external_camp_first_effect = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = { has_variable = dau_choice_pending }
            domicile = { NOT = { has_variable = dau_defer_external_east_asian_estate } }
            OR = { dau_page_external_east_asian_estate_0_trigger = yes dau_page_external_east_asian_estate_1_trigger = yes dau_page_external_east_asian_estate_2_trigger = yes }

        domicile = { save_scope_as = dau_choice_home set_variable = { name = dau_status value = 6 } }
        dau_open_external_east_asian_estate_first_effect = yes
    }

            NOT = { has_variable = dau_choice_pending }
            domicile = { NOT = { has_variable = dau_defer_external_ecclesiastical_domicile } }
            OR = { dau_page_external_ecclesiastical_domicile_0_trigger = yes dau_page_external_ecclesiastical_domicile_1_trigger = yes }

dau_choice_after_effect = {
    # Execute after the option resolves; tooltip previews cannot read selector temporaries.
    if = { limit = { has_variable = dau_choice_selected }
        if = {
            limit = { dau_choice_context_trigger = yes }
            dau_execute_choice_effect = yes
        }
        remove_variable = dau_choice_selected
    }
    if = { limit = { has_variable = dau_choice_navigating } remove_variable = dau_choice_navigating }
    else = {
        remove_variable = dau_choice_pending
        dau_clear_temporary_effect = yes
        if = { limit = { domicile ?= { this = scope:dau_choice_home var:dau_status = 6 } }
            domicile = { set_variable = { name = dau_status value = 0 } }
        }
    }
}
